libraries=standard
type=file
~~~
# SOURCE
~~~sysml
package Guards {
    private import ScalarValues::*;
    part def Ctrl {
        attribute count : Integer;
        attribute ready : Boolean;
        state def S {
            entry; then idle;
            state idle;
            state busy;
            transition t1 first idle if count then busy;
            transition t2 first idle if count + 1 then busy;
            transition t3 first idle if 1 then busy;
            transition t4 first idle if ready then busy;
        }
        action def A {
            action a1;
            action a2;
            first a1 if 42 then a2;
            first a2 if count then a1;
        }
        constraint c1 { count }
        assert constraint c2 { count + 1 }
        assert constraint c3 { 1 }
        requirement r1 { require constraint { count + 1 } }
        attribute n : Real = 2 [SI::kg];
        attribute g = 1 + "a";
        part ps[*..2];
        part pc[0..2];
        part pd[1..3] :> pc;
    }
}
~~~
